

send_child_to_holy_order_tooltip_effect = {
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = order_member
		add_piety_level = 1
		custom_tooltip = character_will_leave_court_holy_order
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			every_spouse = {
				divorce = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = betrothed
			}
			break_betrothal = betrothed
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_been_promised_grand_wedding = yes
				}
				break_grand_wedding_betrothal_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_concubine = yes
			}
			this.concubinist = {
				remove_concubine = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				number_of_concubines > 0
			}
			every_concubine = {
				scope:recipient = {
					remove_concubine = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

send_child_to_holy_order_effect = {
	$GRANDMASTER$ = {
		add_courtier = scope:recipient
		if = {
            limit = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    is_close_family_of = scope:actor
                    NOT = {
                    	has_trait = bastard
                    }
                }
            }
            add_opinion = {
                target = scope:actor
                modifier = pleased_opinion
                opinion = 25
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    is_close_family_of = scope:actor
                }
            }
            add_opinion = {
                target = scope:actor
                modifier = pleased_opinion
                opinion = 15
            }
        }
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = order_member
		add_piety_level = 1
		if = {
			limit = { is_ruler = yes }
			depose = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith != $GRANDMASTER$.faith
			}
			set_character_faith = $GRANDMASTER$.faith
			set_character_rite = $GRANDMASTER$.rite
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			every_spouse = {
				divorce = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = betrothed
			}
			break_betrothal = betrothed
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_been_promised_grand_wedding = yes
				}
				break_grand_wedding_betrothal_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_concubine = yes
			}
			this.concubinist = {
				remove_concubine = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				number_of_concubines > 0
			}
			every_concubine = {
				scope:recipient = {
					remove_concubine = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
	take_vows_faith_opinion_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			$GRANDMASTER$ = {
				has_realm_law = cultural_holy_order_succession_law
				NOT = {
					culture = {
						has_same_culture_heritage = scope:recipient.culture
					}
				}
			}
		}
		$GRANDMASTER$ = { save_scope_as = grandmaster }
		custom_tooltip = military_order_wrong_culture_tt
	}
}

send_child_to_clergy_effect = {
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = pam_simony_paid_vows }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:demand ?= yes
						AND = {
							is_imprisoned_by = scope:puppet_or_actor
							OR = {
								is_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
								any_claim = {
									exists = this
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = forced_taking_vows
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = demanded_taking_vows
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
		}
		simplified_clergy_effect = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		#Spiritual fulfillment gain
   		if = {
   			limit = {
   				scope:recipient = {
    	            is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	            NOT = {
    	        		has_trait = bastard
    	        	}
    	        }
   			}
   			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   				value = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   				if = {
   					limit = {
   						scope:recipient = {
   							is_imprisoned = yes
   						}
   					}
   					multiply = 0.5
   				}
   			}
   		}
   		else_if = {
   			limit = {
   				scope:recipient = {
    	            trigger_if = {
    	            	limit = {
    	            		has_trait = bastard
    	            	}
    	            	is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	            }
    	            OR = {
    	            	house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
    	            	is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	            }
    	        }
   			}
   			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   				value = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   				if = {
   					limit = {
   						scope:recipient = {
   							is_imprisoned = yes
   						}
   					}
   					multiply = 0.5
   				}
   			}
   		}
   		else_if = {
   			limit = {
   				scope:recipient = {
    	            trigger_if = {
    	            	limit = {
    	            		has_trait = bastard
    	            	}
    	            	OR = {
    	            		house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
    	            		is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	           		}
    	            }
    	            trigger_else = {
    	            	OR = {
    	            		dynasty ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
    	            		is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	            	}
    	            }
    	        }
   			}
   			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   				value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   				if = {
   					limit = {
   						scope:recipient = {
   							is_imprisoned = yes
   						}
   					}
   					multiply = 0.5
   				}
   			}
   		}
   		else = {
   			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   				value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   				multiply = 0.5
   				if = {
   					limit = {
   						scope:recipient = {
   							is_imprisoned = yes
   						}
   					}
   					multiply = 0.5
   				}
   			}
   		}
	}
}



simplified_clergy_effect = {
	$RECIPIENT$ = {
		# Full monastic vows supersede a deacon's ordination
		remove_trait = cleric
		add_trait = devoted
		add_piety_level = 1
		if = {
			limit = { is_ruler = yes }
			depose = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			every_spouse = {
				divorce = $RECIPIENT$
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = betrothed
			}
			break_betrothal = betrothed
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_been_promised_grand_wedding = yes
				}
				break_grand_wedding_betrothal_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_concubine = yes
			}
			this.concubinist = {
				remove_concubine = $RECIPIENT$
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				number_of_concubines > 0
			}
			every_concubine = {
				$RECIPIENT$ = {
					remove_concubine = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

release_as_executioner_effect = {
	scope:actor = {
		court_position_grant_effect = {
			EMPLOYER = scope:actor
			POS = executioner
			CANDIDATE = scope:recipient
		}
		save_scope_as = liege
	}
}

create_monastic_decision_effect = {
	save_scope_as = root_scope
	custom_tooltip = create_monastic_order_decision_effect_message_tenet
	custom_tooltip = create_monastic_order_decision_effect_message
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_realm_law = church_authority_1
				has_realm_law = church_authority_2
				has_realm_law = church_authority_3
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = create_monastic_order_gain_hook
	}
	custom_tooltip = create_monastic_order_decision_effect_message_warning

	change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
	stress_impact = {
		zealous = minor_stress_impact_loss
		impatient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		patient = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
		calm = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
	}

	if = { # If you have no valid barony this will throw errors when you open the decision - we add this check to avoid that 
		limit = {
			exists = scope:title
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:title = {
				ordered_county_province = {
					order_by = {
						value = 0
						if = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									terrain = hills
									terrain = mountains
									terrain = desert_mountains
									terrain = desert
								}
							}
							add = 2
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									terrain = forest
									terrain = drylands
									terrain = wetlands
									terrain = jungle
									terrain = terraced_hills
								}
							}
							add = 1
						}
					}
					limit = {
						has_holding = no
						has_ongoing_construction = no
					}
					begin_create_holding = church_holding
					save_scope_as = province
					set_variable = {
						name = monastic_order_capital
						value = root.faith
						years = 50
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_event = holy_order.2009
}

create_monastic_order_leader_and_notification_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:chosen_leader
		}
		scope:chosen_leader = {
			save_scope_as = new_monastic_leader
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:leader = { save_scope_as = new_monastic_leader }
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { # set up currencies for the HO leader, has to be saved as scope:leader
			limit = {
				exists = scope:new_monastic_leader
			}
			scope:new_monastic_leader = {
				set_variable = founding_monastic
			}
			scope:new_holy_order.leader.primary_title = {
				set_variable = {
					name = founder
					value = scope:founder
				}
			}
			#If we have to transfer Order from default leader to someone else
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:new_monastic_leader = {
						has_character_flag = created_char
					}
				}
				scope:new_monastic_leader = {
					remove_character_flag = created_char
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						scope:new_holy_order.leader ?= scope:new_monastic_leader 
					}
				}
				scope:new_holy_order.leader.primary_title = {
					create_title_and_vassal_change = {
						type = granted
						save_scope_as = change
						add_claim_on_loss = yes
					}
					change_title_holder_include_vassals = {
						holder = scope:new_monastic_leader
						change = scope:change
					}
					resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				}
				scope:new_monastic_leader = {
					every_courtier = {
						add_trait = devoted
					}
				}
				
			}
			
			scope:new_monastic_leader = {
				add_gold = 100 #So that they have some money to lend out
				add_piety_level = 2
				add_gold = holy_order_starting_gold
			}
			every_in_list = {
				list = grabbed_monks
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						this = scope:new_monastic_leader
						is_courtier_of = scope:new_monastic_leader
					}
				}
				scope:new_monastic_leader = { add_courtier = prev }
				remove_character_flag = created_char
			}
			
			# send messages
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = monastic_holy_order_founded_message
				desc = i_created_monastic_holy_order_message
				custom_tooltip = monastic_order_tenet_advocated_tt
				left_icon = scope:new_monastic_leader
				right_icon = scope:new_holy_order.title
			}
		}
		# send messages to relevant characters that HO got created
		save_scope_as = holy_order_creator
		every_player = {
			limit = {
				faith = scope:holy_order_creator.faith
				this != scope:holy_order_creator
			}
			send_interface_message = {
				type = monastic_holy_order_founded_message
				desc = someone_created_monastic_holy_order_message
				custom_tooltip = monastic_order_tenet_advocated_tt
				left_icon = scope:holy_order_creator
				right_icon = scope:new_holy_order.title
			}
		}
	}
}

create_monastic_order_founder_effect = {
	# gain opinion with your HoF, if you have one and it's not you
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = faith.religious_head
			faith.religious_head != root
		}
		faith.religious_head = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = founded_monastic_order_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
	}
	# get a nice modifier
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = founded_monastic_order_modifier }
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = founded_monastic_order_modifier
			years = 5
		}
	}
	# boost the fervor
	faith = {
		change_fervor = {
			value = notable_fervor_gain
			desc = fervor_gain_monastic_order_founded
		}
	}

	change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value

	scope:leader = {
		save_scope_as = order_master
	}

	create_character_memory = {
		type = founded_monastic_order_memory
		participants = {
			order_master = scope:leader
		}
	}

	# PAM - Catalysts
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			religion = religion:christianity_religion
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_founded_monastic_order
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_founded_monastic_order
		}
	}
}

grab_best_monk_for_tenet_effect = {
	$TENET$ = {
		save_scope_as = monk_grabbin_tenet
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = dynasty
		}
		dynasty = {
			ordered_dynasty_member = {
				order_by = learning
				#Family member courtier
				limit = {
					is_close_family_of = scope:founder
					house ?= scope:founder.house
					is_courtier_of = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						rite = {
							rite_has_tenet = scope:monk_grabbin_tenet
						}
						has_personal_tenet = scope:monk_grabbin_tenet
					}
				}
				#Family member
				alternative_limit = {
					is_close_family_of = scope:founder
					house ?= scope:founder.house
					is_ruler = no
					in_diplomatic_range = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
					#Don't steal players' courtiers
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							is_courtier = yes
							NOT = {
								is_courtier_of = scope:founder
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							liege = {
								is_ai = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
				#House member courtier
				alternative_limit = {
					house ?= scope:founder.house
					is_courtier_of = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
				}
				#House member
				alternative_limit = {
					house ?= scope:founder.house
					is_ruler = no
					in_diplomatic_range = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
					#Don't steal players' courtiers
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							is_courtier = yes
							NOT = {
								is_courtier_of = scope:founder
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							liege = {
								is_ai = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
				#dynasty member courtier
				alternative_limit = {
					is_courtier_of = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
				}
				#Dynasty member
				alternative_limit = {
					is_ruler = no
					in_diplomatic_range = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
					#Don't steal players' courtiers
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							is_courtier = yes
							NOT = {
								is_courtier_of = scope:founder
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							liege = {
								is_ai = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:abbot_prospect
			}
		}
		#Grab monk courtiers ordered by family importance
		ordered_courtier = {
			order_by = learning
			#Bastards first
			limit = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:founder
				potential_abbot_trigger = yes
			}
			#friends
			alternative_limit = {
				has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:founder }
				potential_abbot_trigger = yes
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				potential_abbot_trigger = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:abbot_prospect
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			#male monk that belongs to local culture
			2 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = female_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = male
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
			#male lowborn monk that belongs to local culture
			2 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = female_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					dynasty = none
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = male
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
			#male lowborn monk that belongs to founder culture
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = female_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					dynasty = none
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = male
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
			#male monk that belongs to founder culture
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = female_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = male
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
			#female nun that belongs to local culture
			2 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = male_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = female
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}

			#female nun that belongs to founder culture
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = male_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = female
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
		}
		scope:abbot_prospect = {
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = created_char
				days = 60
			}
		}
	}
	scope:abbot_prospect = {
		add_to_list = grabbed_monks
	}
}
pick_monastic_founder_option_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:monastic_order
		}
		custom_tooltip = monastic_tenet_pick_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_tooltip = monastic_tenet_pick_unformed_order_tt
	}
	# holy_order.2009 stored the founder boost on the province until this order title existed
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:monastic_order
			scope:barony.title_province ?= { has_variable = new_order_ascetic_boost }
		}
		scope:barony.title_province = {
			var:new_order_ascetic_boost = { save_temporary_scope_as = new_order_ascetic_founder }
			remove_variable = new_order_ascetic_boost
		}
		scope:monastic_order = {
			set_variable = {
				name = ascetic_order_founder
				value = scope:new_order_ascetic_founder
				years = 50
			}
		}
	}
	#Gain popularity if taking holding from old order
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:target_order_tenet
		}
		faith = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
				if = {
					limit = {
						list_size = {
							name = revoked_leases
							value >= 5
						}
					}
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 4
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						list_size = {
							name = revoked_leases
							value >= 4
						}
					}
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 3
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						list_size = {
							name = revoked_leases
							value >= 3
						}
					}
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 2
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						list_size = {
							name = revoked_leases
							value >= 2
						}
					}
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 1.5
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				rite = {
					OR = {
						has_tenet_status = { tenet = tenet_dulia status = core }
						has_tenet_status = { tenet = tenet_dulia status = permitted }
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = monastic_founder_become_saint_tt
		}
		#Reward high church authority theocrat with a hook
		if = {
			limit = {
				root = {
					OR = {
						has_realm_law = church_authority_1
						has_realm_law = church_authority_2
						has_realm_law = church_authority_3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		root = {
			add_hook = {
				type = indebted_hook
				target = scope:chosen_leader
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:target_lessee
		}
		scope:target_lessee = {
			set_relation_rival = {
				target = scope:chosen_leader
				reason = rival_shattered_order
			}
		}
	}
}

take_vows_demand_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:recipient = {
				is_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		add_tyranny = medium_tyranny_gain
	}
	
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
				OR = {
					ai_zeal <= low_negative_ai_value
					ai_greed >= low_positive_ai_value
					ai_vengefulness >= low_positive_ai_value
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = forgiving
					has_trait = craven
					has_trait = zealous
					has_trait = content
					has_trait = humble
					has_trait = bastard
				}
			}
			set_relation_rival = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				reason = rival_forced_to_take_vows
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					#Bastards with decently matching personality don't see this as betrayal
					NAND = {
						has_trait = bastard
						ai_vengefulness < low_positive_ai_value
						ai_greed < low_positive_ai_value
						ai_zeal > low_negative_ai_value
					}
				}
				progress_towards_rival_effect = {
					CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor
					REASON = rival_forced_to_take_vows
					OPINION = 0
				}
			}
		}
		every_parent = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					this = scope:puppet_or_actor
					#Doesn't have other kid in root's line succession
					any_child = {
						NOT = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
						is_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					has_trait = zealous
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = forced_child_take_vows
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
}

send_child_to_monastic_order_effect = {
   	# Legitimacy loss if you're doing this to your children
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			scope:recipient = { is_primary_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				hyperdulia_take_the_vows_trigger = yes
			}
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = miniscule_legitimacy_gain
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = minor_legitimacy_loss
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			scope:recipient = { is_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				hyperdulia_take_the_vows_trigger = yes
			}
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = minor_legitimacy_gain
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = miniscule_legitimacy_loss
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			hyperdulia_take_the_vows_trigger = yes
		}
		add_legitimacy = {
			value = medium_legitimacy_gain
			multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
		}
	}

   	#Spiritual fulfillment gain
   	if = {
   		limit = {
   			scope:recipient = {
                is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                NOT = {
            		has_trait = bastard
            	}
            }
   		}
   		change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   			value = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   			if = {
   				limit = {
   					scope:recipient = {
   						is_imprisoned = yes
   					}
   				}
   				multiply = 0.5
   			}
   		}
   	}
   	else_if = {
   		limit = {
   			scope:recipient = {
                trigger_if = {
                	limit = {
                		has_trait = bastard
                	}
                	is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                }
                OR = {
                	house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
                	is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                }
            }
   		}
   		change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   			value = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   			if = {
   				limit = {
   					scope:recipient = {
   						is_imprisoned = yes
   					}
   				}
   				multiply = 0.5
   			}
   		}
   	}
   	else_if = {
   		limit = {
   			scope:recipient = {
                trigger_if = {
                	limit = {
                		has_trait = bastard
                	}
                	OR = {
                		house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
                		is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
               		}
                }
                trigger_else = {
                	OR = {
                		dynasty ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
                		is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                	}
                }
            }
   		}
   		change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   			value = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   			if = {
   				limit = {
   					scope:recipient = {
   						is_imprisoned = yes
   					}
   				}
   				multiply = 0.5
   			}
   		}
   	}
   	else = {
   		change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   			value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   			if = {
   				limit = {
   					scope:recipient = {
   						is_imprisoned = yes
   					}
   				}
   				multiply = 0.5
   			}
   		}
   	}
   

    #Send to holy order if we can, if not... make just rando monk in a bishop's court
    scope:archabbot = {
        add_courtier = scope:recipient
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                    NOT = {
                    	has_trait = bastard
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                }
            }
            add_opinion = {
                target = scope:puppet_or_actor
                modifier = pleased_opinion
                opinion = 15
            }
        }
        if = {
        	limit = {
        		scope:recipient = {
        			OR = {
                		house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
                		is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
               		}
        		}
        	}
        	dynastic_monks_gain_xp_effect = yes
        }
    }

    scope:recipient = {
        add_trait = devoted
        add_piety_level = 1
        if = {
            limit = { is_ruler = yes }
            depose = yes
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_married = yes
            }
            every_spouse = {
                divorce = scope:recipient
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = betrothed
            }
            break_betrothal = betrothed
            if = {
                limit = {
                    has_been_promised_grand_wedding = yes
                }
                break_grand_wedding_betrothal_effect = yes
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_concubine = yes
            }
            this.concubinist = {
                remove_concubine = scope:recipient
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                number_of_concubines > 0
            }
            every_concubine = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    remove_concubine = prev
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            house = {
                has_house_aspiration_parameter = humility_take_the_vows_bonus
            }
        }
        add_piety_experience = 100
        add_character_modifier = {
            modifier = tgp_humility_take_the_vows_bonus_modifier
            years = 10
        }
    }
}
take_vows_faith_opinion_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				NOT = {
              		has_trait = bastard
              	}
			}
		}
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = pious_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
	}
	#bastards
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = pious_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = faith.religious_head
			faith.religious_head != scope:puppet_or_actor
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				NOT = {
              		has_trait = bastard
              	}
			}
		}
		faith.religious_head = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = pious_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
	}
}
monastic_lifestyle_xp_yearly_gain_effect = {
	 
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_5
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_4
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.9
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_3
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.8
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_2
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.7
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_1
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.6
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.3
		}
	}
	#Stewardship
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_5
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.8
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_4
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.7
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_3
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.6
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_2
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.5
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_1
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.4
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.2
		}
	}
}

military_order_yearly_trait_gain_effect = {
	#Lazarites might gain a little leprosy...
	if = {
		limit = {
			primary_title = title:d_knights_of_st_lazarus
		}
		random = {
			chance = 10
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = leper }
				}
				add_to_list = lazarists
			}
			every_courtier = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = leper }
				}
			}
			random_in_list = {
				list = lazarists
				add_trait = leper
			}
		}
	}
	#Hospital orders gain physician trait XP
	if = {
		limit = {
			primary_title = {
				OR = {
					this = title:d_knights_of_st_lazarus
					this = title:d_knights_hospitaller
					this = title:d_knights_of_the_tau
				}
			}
		}
		every_courtier = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = lifestyle_physician
			}
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_physician
				value = { 1 10 }
			}
		}
		random = {
			chance = 50
			ordered_courtier = {
				order_by = learning
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = lifestyle_physician
					}
				}
				add_trait = lifestyle_physician
			}
		}
	}
}
set_up_historical_monastic_orders_effect = {
	#BENEDICTINES
	#Store-brand Benedictines
	if = {
		limit = {
			#Benedictines in Italy are eternal
			always = yes
		}
		title:b_cento = {
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				current_year < 868
			}
			character:benedictine_1 = {
				save_scope_as = monastic_leader
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				current_year < 1067
			}
			character:benedictine_3 = {
				save_scope_as = monastic_leader
			}
		}
		else = {
			character:benedictine_4 = {
				save_scope_as = monastic_leader
			}
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			#Add now-unneeded abbots as courtiers
			if = {
				limit = {
					current_year < 868
				}
				add_courtier = character:pastoral_benedictine_1
				add_courtier = character:anianian_benedictine_1
				add_courtier = character:maurian_benedictine_1
				add_courtier = character:gallic_benedictine_1
				add_courtier = character:168025
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					current_year < 1067
				}
				add_courtier = character:edgaric_1
				add_courtier = character:corbine_benedictine_2
				add_courtier = character:gurloesian_1
				add_courtier = character:ripollese_1
				add_courtier = character:sankt_gallen_1
				add_courtier = character:ottonian_benedictine_1
				add_courtier = character:glagolitic_1
				add_courtier = character:guntherian_benedictine_1
			}
			else = {
				add_courtier = character:guntherian_benedictine_2
			}
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = devoted
					value = { 20 80 }
				}
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:monastic_leader
					}
					save_scope_as = benedictine
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		#867 ONLY
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:benedictine
				current_year < 868
			}
			#ITALY
			title:b_montalcino = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }
			title:b_farfa = {
				holder.liege = { get_title = prev } # grab from theocrat baron vassal
				lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine
			}
			title:b_cassino = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }

			#BRITAIN
			#English monasteries that were doing something more or less benedictine
			title:b_saint_albans = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }
			title:b_hexham = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }
			title:b_wolverhampton = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }
			title:b_whitby = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }

			#Only here until Cluniac/Gorziac reforms:
			#AQUITAINE
			#Represent Abbey of St Saveur
			title:b_lodeve = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }
			title:b_figeac = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }
			
			#HRE
			#Hallein was held by Abbey of St Peter in Salzburg
			title:b_hallein = {
				holder.liege = { get_title = prev } # grab from theocrat baron vassal
				lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine
			}
			title:b_calw = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine }
			#represents great abbey of fulda
			title:b_hersfeld = {
				holder.liege = { get_title = prev } # grab from theocrat baron vassal
				lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine
			}
			#Lorsch abbey
			title:b_besensheim = {
				holder.liege = { get_title = prev } # grab from theocrat baron vassal
				lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:benedictine
			}

	##Columban Benedictines
	#Integrated into Benedictine rule gradually
	if = {
		limit = {
			current_date < 868.1.1
		}
		title:b_sankt_gallen = {
			holder.liege = { get_title = prev } # grab from theocrat baron vassal
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
		}
		title:c_sankt_gallen.holder = {
			save_scope_as = game_start_founder
			set_variable = {
				name = monastic_game_start_founder
				days = 10
			}
		}
		character:columban_benedictine_1 = {
			save_scope_as = monastic_leader
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			add_courtier = character:corbine_benedictine_1
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = devoted
					value = { 20 80 }
				}
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:monastic_leader
					}
					save_scope_as = columban_benedictine
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:columban_benedictine
			}
			#Columban founde monasteries here and nearby
			title:b_lure = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine }
			#Representing Bobbio
			title:b_florentiola = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine }
			title:b_pomposa = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine }

			#Columban influence had faded, but generic benedictines are a bit bloated so...
			title:b_dassel = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine }
			title:b_corbie = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine }

			random_faith_holy_order = {
				limit = {
					leader = scope:monastic_leader
				}
				save_scope_as = rightful_dayroye
			}
		}
	}
	#Lease out additional holdings
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:rightful_dayroye
		}
		title:b_kafartutha = {
			holder.liege = { get_title = prev } # grab from theocrat baron vassal
			lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:rightful_dayroye
		}
	}

	##Community of St Catherine
	#The monks of Mount Sinai
	title:b_stcatherine = {
		holder.liege = { get_title = prev } # grab from theocrat baron vassal
		save_scope_as = monastic_barony
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			current_year < 1066
		}
		character:community_of_st_catherine_1 = {
			save_scope_as = monastic_leader
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			current_year < 1178
		}
		character:community_of_st_catherine_2 = {
			save_scope_as = monastic_leader
		}
	}
	else = {
		character:community_of_st_catherine_3 = {
			save_scope_as = monastic_leader
		}
	}
	title:c_sinai.holder = {
		save_scope_as = game_start_founder
		set_variable = {
			name = monastic_game_start_founder
			days = 10
		}
	}
	scope:game_start_founder = {
		set_variable = {
			name = community_of_st_catherine_founder
			days = 10
		}
		create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
			LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
			CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
			NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
			FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
			ORDER_TYPE = monastic
		}
		remove_variable = community_of_st_catherine_founder
	}
	scope:monastic_leader = {
		every_courtier = {
			add_trait = devoted
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = devoted
				value = { 20 80 }
			}
		}
	}

	#Give all monastic leaders some monastic trait XP
	every_religion_global = {
		every_faith = {
			limit = {
				any_faith_holy_order = {
					exists = this	
				}	
			}
			every_faith_holy_order = {
				limit = {
					leader = {
						has_trait = devoted
					}
				}
				leader = {
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = devoted
						value = { 40 90 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
}

dynastic_monks_gain_xp_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:puppet_or_actor } }
		scope:actor = { save_scope_as = puppet_or_actor }
	}
	every_courtier = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = devoted
			OR = {
				AND = {
					exists = scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
					dynasty ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
				}
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		add_trait_xp = {
			trait = devoted
			value = {
				value = 2
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					add = 2
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
					}
					add = 2
				}
				#increase for lease granting interaction
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:target
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
				#Increase for huge donation
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:huge_donation ?= yes
					}
					multiply = 2.5
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
#Holy Order patronage loss warning
grant_title_holy_order_patronage_warning_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			#Holy order is actually involved
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_patroned_holy_order = {
					leader.capital_county ?= {
    					save_temporary_scope_as = ho_capital_temp
    					any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
    						OR = {
    							is_in_list = target_titles
    							holder ?= {
    								target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:recipient
    							}
    						}
    					}
					}
    			}
			}
			#Duchy tier or higher is involved
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {	
					#Is duke
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					#Will be duke and either already controls duchy or will
					AND = {
						any_in_list = {
							list = target_titles
							tier >= tier_duchy
						}
						OR = {
							scope:ho_capital_temp.holder ?= {
								target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:recipient
							}
							any_in_list = {
								list = target_titles
								this = scope:ho_capital_temp
							}
							AND = {
								scope:ho_capital_temp.holder.highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
								any_in_list = {
									list = target_titles
									tier >= tier_duchy
									any_de_jure_county = {
										this = scope:ho_capital_temp
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = holy_order_control_loss_warning_tt
	}
	#Potential holy order patronage loss warning
	if = {
		limit = {
			#No duchies involved
			NOR = {
				scope:recipient = {
					highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
				}
				any_in_list = {
					list = target_titles
					tier > tier_county
				}
			}
			#But you are giving away Order capital
			scope:actor = {
				any_patroned_holy_order = {
					leader.capital_county ?= {
    					save_temporary_scope_as = ho_capital_temp
    					is_in_list = target_titles
					}
    			}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = potential_holy_order_control_loss_warning_tt
	}
}

monastic_belief_spreading_targeting_effect = {
	save_scope_as = holy_order_leader
	holy_order = {
		save_scope_as = holy_order
	}
	holy_order.order_tenet = {
		save_scope_as = order_tenet
	}
	rite = {
		save_scope_as = order_rite
	}
	#Make lists of targets
	holy_order = {
		#Spread Tenet to AI characters as personal tenet
		every_leased_title = {
			#leases' duchies' counties
			duchy ?= {
				every_de_jure_county_holder = {
					limit = {
						pam_monastic_tenet_spread_target_trigger = { ORDER_LEADER = scope:holy_order_leader }
					}
					add_to_list = personal_tenet_targets
				}
			}
			#Neighboring counties to leases
			county ?= {
				every_neighboring_county = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							holder ?= {
								pam_monastic_tenet_spread_target_trigger = { ORDER_LEADER = scope:holy_order_leader }
							}
						}
						holder = {
							add_to_list = personal_tenet_targets
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#Change character tenets
	every_in_list = {
		list = personal_tenet_targets
		#Of AI
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = {
					value = {
						value = scope:holy_order_leader.learning
						multiply = 0.5
					}
					#Actually want tenet
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								any_desired_tenet = {
									selection_count = 97
									only_positive = yes
									threshold = 1
									this = scope:order_tenet
								}
							}
						}
						add = 0.1
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_desired_tenet = {
								selection_count = 1
								only_positive = yes
								this = scope:order_tenet
							}
						}
						multiply = 5
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_desired_tenet = {
								selection_count = 3
								only_positive = yes
								this = scope:order_tenet
							}
						}
						multiply = 3
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_desired_tenet = {
								selection_count = 6
								only_positive = yes
								this = scope:order_tenet
							}
						}
						multiply = 2
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							rite = {
								has_tenet_status = {
									tenet = scope:order_tenet
									status = core
								}
							}
						}
						multiply = 2
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							rite = {
								has_tenet_status = {
									tenet = scope:order_tenet
									status = permitted
								}
							}
						}
						multiply = 1.5
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							rite = {
								has_tenet_status = {
									tenet = scope:order_tenet
									status = prohibited
								}
							}
						}
						multiply = 0.25
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								capital_county.kingdom = scope:holy_order_leader.capital_county.kingdom
							}
						}
						multiply = 0.75
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								capital_county.empire = scope:holy_order_leader.capital_county.empire
							}
						}
						multiply = 0.75
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
						}
						multiply = 0.25
					}
				}
				add_to_list = order_tenet_accepted
			}
		}
	}
}

monastic_tenet_espousing_effect = {
	add_character_flag = {
		flag = mon_tenet_cooldown
		years = 5
	}
	#Need to convince at least someone, jeez...
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_in_list = {
					count >= 1
					list = order_tenet_accepted
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
		}
		random_in_list = {
			list = personal_tenet_targets
			limit = {
				highest_held_title_tier <= tier_county
				is_ai = yes
			}
			limit = {
				highest_held_title_tier <= tier_duchy
				is_ai = yes
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
			add_to_list = order_tenet_accepted
		}
	}
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = order_tenet_accepted_count
		value = list_size:order_tenet_accepted
	}
	

	#APPLY TO CHARACTERS SELECTED IN monastic_belief_spreading_targeting_effect
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_in_list = {
				list = order_tenet_accepted
				count >= 1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = order_tenet_accepted_list_tt
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		every_in_list = {
			list = order_tenet_accepted
			add_character_flag = free_tenet_change
			force_set_personal_tenet = scope:order_tenet
			remove_character_flag = free_tenet_change

		}
	}
	
	#players choose via interaction
	every_in_list = {
		list = personal_tenet_targets
		limit = {
			is_ai = no
		}
		save_scope_as = tenet_interaction_recipient
		run_interaction = {
			interaction = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction
			actor = scope:holy_order_leader
			recipient = scope:tenet_interaction_recipient
			execute_threshold = decline
		}
		add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
	}
	#give some correct rite courtiers piety
	custom_tooltip = espouse_beliefs_monks_piety_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_piety = medium_piety_gain
		every_courtier = {
			limit = {
				rite = scope:order_rite
				has_trait = devoted
			}
			random = {
				chance = {
					value = scope:holy_order_leader.learning
					multiply = 4
				}
				add_piety = medium_piety_gain
			}
		}
		#Set courtier rite to order rite
		every_courtier = {
			limit = {
				faith = scope:holy_order_leader.faith
				NOT = {
					rite = scope:order_rite
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = {
					value = scope:holy_order_leader.learning
					multiply = 4
				}
				set_character_rite = scope:holy_order_leader.rite
			}
		}
	}
	scope:holy_order_leader = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = devoted }
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = devoted
				value = 5
			}
		}
	}

	#Inform their lease holders of what happened
	holy_order = {
		every_leased_title = {
			county.holder ?= {
				add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
			}
		}
	}
	faith = {
		every_faith_playable_ruler = {
			limit = {
				is_clergy = yes
				in_diplomatic_range = scope:holy_order_leader
			}
			add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		every_in_list = {
			list = rulers_to_notify
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
			}
			save_scope_as = msg_receiver
			#Personal Tenet spread message
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_in_list = {
						list = order_tenet_accepted
						count > 2
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
				}
				ordered_in_list = {
					list = order_tenet_accepted
					order_by = highest_held_title_tier
					limit = {
						is_liege_or_above_of = scope:msg_receiver
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						OR = {
							house ?= scope:msg_receiver.house
							has_any_scripted_relation = scope:msg_receiver
						}
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						liege ?= scope:msg_receiver
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						top_liege ?= scope:msg_receiver
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						any_character_to_title_neighboring_county = {
						 	holder = scope:msg_receiver
						}
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = tenet_change_1
				}
				ordered_in_list = {
					list = order_tenet_accepted
					order_by = highest_held_title_tier
					limit = {
						is_liege_or_above_of = scope:msg_receiver
						NOR = {
							this = scope:tenet_change_1
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						OR = {
							house ?= scope:msg_receiver.house
							has_any_scripted_relation = scope:msg_receiver
						}
						NOR = {
							this = scope:tenet_change_1
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						liege ?= scope:msg_receiver
						NOR = {
							this = scope:tenet_change_1
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						top_liege ?= scope:msg_receiver
						NOR = {
							this = scope:tenet_change_1
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						any_character_to_title_neighboring_county = {
						 	holder = scope:msg_receiver
						}
						NOR = {
							this = scope:tenet_change_1
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					alternative_limit = {
						NOR = {
							this = scope:tenet_change_1
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = tenet_change_2
				}
				send_interface_message = {
					type = msg_order_espousing_beliefs
					title = msg_order_espousing_beliefs_title_tenet
					left_icon = scope:tenet_change_1
					right_icon = scope:holy_order_leader.primary_title
					custom_tooltip = order_spreading_tenet_tt
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_in_list = {
						list = order_tenet_accepted
						count = 2
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
				}
				random_in_list = {
					list = order_tenet_accepted
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = tenet_change_1
				}
				random_in_list = {
					list = order_tenet_accepted
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
							this = scope:tenet_change_1
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = tenet_change_2
				}
				send_interface_message = {
					type = msg_order_espousing_beliefs
					title = msg_order_espousing_beliefs_title_tenet
					left_icon = scope:tenet_change_1
					right_icon = scope:holy_order_leader.primary_title
					custom_tooltip = order_spreading_tenet_tt_char_1
					custom_tooltip = order_spreading_tenet_tt_char_2
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_in_list = {
						list = order_tenet_accepted
						count = 1
						NOT = {
							this = scope:msg_receiver
						}
					}
				}
				random_in_list = {
					list = order_tenet_accepted
					save_scope_as = tenet_change_1
				}
				send_interface_message = {
					type = msg_order_espousing_beliefs
					title = msg_order_espousing_beliefs_title_tenet
					left_icon = scope:tenet_change_1
					right_icon = scope:holy_order_leader.primary_title
					custom_tooltip = order_spreading_tenet_tt_char_1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Other effect where they gain monasteries and increase development

#Get monasteries where Rite and ruler tenet matches theirs
#Increase development in matching-rite duchies they lease in
#improve monk courtiers of leader
#Inform their lease holders of what happened
#Inform lease holders of other monasteries they had monasteries switched
monastic_lease_swapping_effect = {
	add_character_flag = {
		flag = mon_monastery_gain_cooldown
		years = 3
	}
	add_character_flag = {
		flag = just_swapped_lease
		days = 10
	}
	save_scope_as = holy_order_leader
	holy_order = {
		save_scope_as = holy_order
	}
	holy_order.order_tenet = {
		save_scope_as = order_tenet
	}
	rite = {
		save_scope_as = order_rite
	}
	faith = {
		random_faith_holy_order = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					this = scope:holy_order
				}
				any_leased_title = {
					monastic_lease_can_be_suborned_trigger = { ORDER = scope:holy_order }
				}
				scope:holy_order_leader.var:old_order ?= this
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				NOT = {
					this = scope:holy_order
				}
				any_leased_title = {
					monastic_lease_can_be_suborned_trigger = { ORDER = scope:holy_order }
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = target_order
			ordered_leased_title = {
				order_by = {
					value = county.development_level
					multiply = -1
				}
				limit = {
					monastic_lease_can_be_suborned_trigger = { ORDER = scope:holy_order }
					NOT = {
						county = scope:target_order.leader.capital_county
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = taken_monastery
			}
			#Capital monastery if only available
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						exists = scope:taken_monastery
					}
				}
				ordered_leased_title = {
					order_by = {
						value = county.development_level
						multiply = -1
					}
					limit = {
						monastic_lease_can_be_suborned_trigger = { ORDER = scope:holy_order }
					}
					save_scope_as = taken_monastery
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:taken_monastery
		}
		#Inform their lease holders of what is happening
		holy_order = {
			every_leased_title = {
				county.holder ?= {
					add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
				}
			}
		}
		faith.religious_head ?= {
			add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
		}
		rite.head_of_rite ?= {
			add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
		}
		scope:target_order = {
			every_leased_title = {
				county.holder ?= {
					add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
				}
			}
			leader.rite.head_of_rite ?= {
				add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
			}
		}
		scope:taken_monastery.county = {
			holder ?= {
				add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
			}
			clerical_region_title.holder ?= {
				add_to_list = rulers_to_notify
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = rulers_to_notify
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
			}
			save_scope_as = msg_receiver
	
			#Normal message
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:target_order = {
						any_leased_title = {
							NOT = {
								this = scope:taken_monastery
							}
						}
					}
				}
				send_interface_message = {
					type = msg_order_took_over_monastery
					title = msg_order_took_over_monastery.t
					left_icon = scope:holy_order_leader.primary_title
					right_icon = scope:target_order.leader.primary_title
					custom_tooltip = order_took_over_monastery_tt
					if = {
						limit = {
							#Rite mismatch caused this
							scope:target_order.leader.rite ?= {
								NOT = {
									this = scope:order_rite
								}
								OR = {
									NOT = {
										scope:taken_monastery.county.clerical_region_title.holder.rite ?= scope:target_order.leader.rite
									}
									NOT = {
										scope:taken_monastery.county.holder.rite ?= this
									}
								}
							}
						}
						scope:target_order.leader.rite = { save_scope_as = target_rite }
						custom_tooltip = monastic_spreading_rite_tt
					}
					#Influence of patron or puppeteer
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							scope:taken_monastery.county = {
								OR = {
									clerical_region_title.holder ?= {
										OR = {
											scope:holy_order.holy_order_patron ?= this
											scope:holy_order_leader.puppeteer ?= this
											any_personal_tenet = {
												this = scope:order_tenet
											}
										}
									}
									holder ?= {
										OR = {
											scope:holy_order.holy_order_patron ?= this
											scope:holy_order_leader.puppeteer ?= this
											any_personal_tenet = {
												this = scope:order_tenet
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								scope:taken_monastery.county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= {
									OR = {
										scope:holy_order.holy_order_patron ?= this
										scope:holy_order_leader.puppeteer ?= this
										any_personal_tenet = {
											this = scope:order_tenet
										}
									}
								}
							}
							scope:taken_monastery.county.clerical_region_title.holder = {
								save_scope_as = instigator
							}
						}
						else = {
							scope:taken_monastery.county.holder ?= {
								save_scope_as = instigator
							}
						}
						custom_tooltip = monastic_spreading_instigated_tt
					}
				}
			}
			#Order destroying message
			else = {
				send_interface_message = {
					type = msg_order_took_over_monastery
					title = msg_order_took_over_other_order.t
					left_icon = scope:holy_order_leader.primary_title
					right_icon = scope:target_order.leader.primary_title
					custom_tooltip = order_took_over_capital_monastery_tt
					if = {
						limit = {
							#Rite mismatch caused this
							scope:target_order.leader.rite ?= {
								NOT = {
									this = scope:order_rite
								}
								OR = {
									NOT = {
										scope:taken_monastery.county.clerical_region_title.holder.rite ?= scope:target_order.leader.rite
									}
									NOT = {
										scope:taken_monastery.county.holder.rite ?= this
									}
								}
							}
						}
						scope:target_order.leader.rite = { save_scope_as = target_rite }
						custom_tooltip = monastic_spreading_rite_tt
					}
					#Influence of patron or puppeteer
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							scope:taken_monastery.county = {
								OR = {
									clerical_region_title.holder ?= {
										OR = {
											scope:holy_order.holy_order_patron ?= this
											scope:holy_order_leader.puppeteer ?= this
											any_personal_tenet = {
												this = scope:order_tenet
											}
										}
									}
									holder ?= {
										OR = {
											scope:holy_order.holy_order_patron ?= this
											scope:holy_order_leader.puppeteer ?= this
											any_personal_tenet = {
												this = scope:order_tenet
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								scope:taken_monastery.county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= {
									OR = {
										scope:holy_order.holy_order_patron ?= this
										scope:holy_order_leader.puppeteer ?= this
										any_personal_tenet = {
											this = scope:order_tenet
										}
									}
								}
							}
							scope:taken_monastery.county.clerical_region_title.holder = {
								save_scope_as = instigator
							}
						}
						else = {
							scope:taken_monastery.county.holder ?= {
								save_scope_as = instigator
							}
						}
						custom_tooltip = monastic_spreading_instigated_tt
					}
				}
			}
			
		}
		scope:taken_monastery = {
			revoke_lease = yes
			lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:holy_order
		}
		#Monk courtiers get better
		add_trait_xp = {
			trait = devoted
			value = 10
		}
		every_courtier = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = devoted
			}
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = devoted
				value = 5
			}
		}
	}
}
